铁电体的好选择。2004年，Fong等人报道了在室温下薄至1.2 nm的超薄钙钛矿PbTiO3薄膜中稳定的铁电性。[10]2017年，Gao等人通过定量环形明场成像报道了1.5个原胞的铁电材料PbZr0.2Ti0.8O3（PZT）。[121]2018年，在二维钙钛矿氧化物薄膜中研究了具有三种不同机制的三种铁电态。[122]同年，Ruddlesden–Popper家族（An+1BnX3n+1）成员之一四氯铅酸双（苄基铵）（BA2PbCl4）通过PFM在室温下被证明为铁电体。[123]通过相对于悬臂轴旋转样品，进行了角度分辨PFM以确定薄片的极化矢量。压电响应的大小通过振幅和相位信号的组合显示，从而验证了BA2PbCl4的面内极化轴，如图12a中的箭头所示。在确认单个原胞和两个范德华层薄片中面内铁电性的存在后，使用共面电极测试了铁电极化的可切换性。畴壁不仅可能因来自电极的过量空穴和少量电子注入而不同，而且还可以通过使用偏压AFM探针沿虚线箭头扫描进行光刻（图12b）。铁电性的优异性能归因于偏离中心的有机阳离子以及Pb2+沿极性轴从八面体中心的位移。BA2PbCl4在柔性应用中也表现良好，向上和向下弯曲产生相反符号的电流，使这种二维杂化钙钛矿在非易失性信息处理与存储以及其他创新器件中展现出潜在应用。这一新发现的结果鼓励人们寻求
<span class="hljs-strong">**Ruddlesden–Popper**</span>: 一种层状钙钛矿结构系列，通式为An+1BnX3n+1

a deeper understanding of the suppression of ferroelectricity in perovskite oxide thin films. Instead of exploring the OOP polarization, robust IP ferroelectricity might be the start to obtain unique 2D conventional ferroelectrics. The discovery of weaker thickness-dependent ferroelectric orders should be the important point for breaking the limitation of the size effect. In 2019, 1 u.c. thick tetragonal BiFeO3 ultrathin films at room temperature were demonstrated, which further scaled down the critical thickness for traditional ferroelectric materials. It was suggested that the crystallographic structure, tetragonality, interfacial chemical environments, and ionic polarization of oxide electrode contributed to reducing the ferroelectrics critical thickness, making it promise for high-density memories and miniaturizing devices.[18] In conclusion, although the realistic evidence is not sufficient, the works to date have already opened up new opportunities to observing and understanding ferroelectricity in potential 2D materials, including van der Waals materials and traditional ferroelectrics, which have shed light on the world of 2D ferroelectrics. Group-IV materials, such as chalcogenides and tellirides, play an important role in theoretical 2D ferroelectric materials, which still have rare progress experimentally. The phosphorene structure usually gives these materials more chances to obtain robust 2D ferroelectricity. Additionally, the TMDs family is also a great promising candidate for discovering 2D ferroelectricity. The improper origin or interlayer translation probably makes achieving 2D ferroelectrics easier. However, compared with interlayer translation, intralayer polarization excludes the odd-even number layers limitation. Most 2D ferroelectric materials found experimentally, including SnTe, MoTe2, WTe2, BA2PbCl4, etc., are ferroelectric whether they are odd or even layered. Based on the urgent demand of miniaturization of next-generation memory and logical devices, more experimental observations are required to add new members to the 2D ferroelectric family since approaches to verify the breaking inversion symmetry and the polarization switching are already available. What we discussed here is just the beginning of this rising field.
对钙钛矿氧化物薄膜中铁电性抑制的更深入理解。与其探索面外极化，强大的面内铁电性可能是获得独特二维传统铁电体的起点。发现较弱厚度依赖性的铁电有序应是打破尺寸效应限制的关键点。2019年，展示了在室温下1个原胞厚的四方相BiFeO3超薄膜，进一步缩小了传统铁电材料的临界厚度。研究表明，晶体结构、四方性、界面化学环境和氧化物电极的离子极化有助于降低铁电临界厚度，使其有望用于高密度存储和小型化器件。[18]总之，尽管实际证据尚不充分，但迄今为止的工作已经为观察和理解潜在二维材料（包括范德华材料和传统铁电体）中的铁电性开辟了新的机遇，这为二维铁电体的世界带来了光明。IV族材料，如硫族化物和碲化物，在理论二维铁电材料中发挥着重要作用，但实验上进展仍然稀少。磷烯结构通常赋予这些材料更多获得强大二维铁电性的机会。此外，TMDs家族也是发现二维铁电性的极具前景的候选者。不恰当起源或层间平移可能使实现二维铁电体更容易。然而，与层间平移相比，层内极化排除了奇偶层数的限制。实验上发现的大多数二维铁电材料，包括SnTe、MoTe2、WTe2、BA2PbCl4等，无论奇偶层数都具有铁电性。基于下一代存储器和逻辑器件小型化的迫切需求，需要更多的实验观察来为二维铁电家族增添新成员，因为验证反转对称性破缺和极化反转的方法已经可用。我们在此讨论的只是这一新兴领域的开始。

<span class="hljs-section"># 3. How to Obtain Proofs Indicating 2D Ferroelectricity</span>
如何获得表明二维铁电性的证据

<span class="hljs-section">## 3.1. Calculation Methods to Predict 2D Ferroelectrics</span>
预测二维铁电体的计算方法

Most currently reported simulations on 2D ferroelectricity depend on first principle calculations based on DFT, as implemented in the Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package.[124] The pseudopotentials/ultrasoft pseudopotentials are generated by using the projector-augmented wave with a plane-wave basis set, which is employed for electron–ion interactions.[125] The exchange correlation potential is treated in the Perdew–BurkeErnzerhof form of the generalized gradient approximation or local density approximation to approximate the exchange and correlation potential.[126] For electronic band structure computation, the Brillouin zone is sampled in the Monkhorst–Pack scheme, while the more accurate screened exchange hybrid Heyd–Scuseria–Ernzerhof functional is used.[127] To eliminate the interaction between adjacent monolayers, a sufficiently large vacuum thickness (≈20 Å) along the z axis is always adopted in the theoretical calculations. The polarization switching process (energy barrier of ferroelectric/ferroelastic phase transition) is investigated with the nudged elastic band method.[128] The Berry phase method (based on the Kohn–Sham wave functions from DFT calculations) rigorously defines the spontaneous polarization of a periodic solid and provides a route for its computation in electronic structure codes, which is used in the calculations of the ferroelectric polarization.[129] The DFT+U method is adopted to give a better description of the strongly correlated effect.[130]Inspired by the successful DFT+U method, the orbital selective external potential method,[131] describing an external orbital-independent potential, is proposed to simulate the potential to influence the specific atomic orbit for theoretical purposes. The van der Waals interaction is modeled using the Lennard-Jones potential.[132]
目前大多数关于二维铁电性的模拟都依赖于基于DFT的第一性原理计算，如在Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package中实现的那样。[124]赝势/超软赝势通过使用投影缀加平面波方法产生，并采用平面波基组，用于电子-离子相互作用。[125]交换关联势在广义梯度近似或局域密度近似的Perdew–Burke–Ernzerhof形式中处理，以近似交换和关联势。[126]对于电子能带结构计算，布里渊区在Monkhorst–Pack方案中取样，同时使用更精确的屏蔽交换杂化Heyd–Scuseria–Ernzerhof泛函。[127]为消除相邻单层之间的相互作用，理论计算中通常在z轴方向采用足够大的真空层厚度（约20 Å）。极化反转过程（铁电/铁弹相变的能垒）使用nudged elastic band方法进行研究。[128]Berry相位方法（基于DFT计算中的Kohn–Sham波函数）严格定义了周期固体的自发极化，并为其在电子结构代码中的计算提供了途径，该方法用于铁电极化的计算。[129]采用DFT+U方法以更好地描述强关联效应。[130]受成功的DFT+U方法启发，提出了轨道选择性外势方法，[131]描述一种与轨道无关的外势，以模拟影响特定原子轨道的势，用于理论目的。范德华相互作用使用Lennard-Jones势模拟。[132]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Brillouin zone**</span>: 布里渊区，倒易空间中的原胞
<span class="hljs-strong">**Nudged elastic band method**</span>: 微动弹性带方法，一种寻找过渡态和最小能量路径的计算方法

<span class="hljs-section">## 3.2. Advanced Techniques Proving 2D Ferroelectricity</span>
证明二维铁电性的先进技术

According to the experimental development of 2D ferroelectric materials, it is very challenging to probe the ferroelectric properties in ultrathin films due to the much-reduced vertical signals compared with the bulk, indicating that more sensitive approaches are required to monitor the 2D properties. Generally, more sensitive probes, including synchrotron X-ray scattering,[10] ultraviolet Raman spectroscopy,[133] polarized SHG,[82,134,135] PFM,[136,137] etc., are employed to detect weak signals.[108] Here we briefly introduce four of them.
根据二维铁电材料的实验发展，探测超薄膜中的铁电性质非常具有挑战性，因为与块体相比，垂直信号大大减弱，表明需要更灵敏的方法来监测二维性质。通常，更灵敏的探针包括同步辐射X射线散射、[10]紫外拉曼光谱、[133]偏振SHG、[82,134,135]PFM、[136,137]等，被用于检测弱信号。[108]在此，我们简要介绍其中四种技术。

<span class="hljs-section">### 3.2.1. STM</span>
扫描隧道显微镜

STM provides a direct technique to probe the local electronic structure, and STS could offer information on the electronic density of states of different materials. The principle of the STM is straightforward. It essentially consists of scanning a metal tip over the surface at a constant tunnel current. The displacements of the conductive tip caused by the voltages applied to the piezo drives would yield the picture of the surface topography. The high resolution of the STM strongly depends on the tunnel current on the distance between the two tunnel electrodes, that is, the metal tip and the scanned surface.[138] The STS reveals a robust electronic gap-like feature around the zero bias, as well as significant spatial inhomogeneity at higher biases above the gap edge.[139,140] In fact, this method has been used in many different aspects, including characterizing the inhomogeneous electronic structure,[141] atomic structure,[139,142] electron–phonon/phonon–electron interaction,[143] the effect of twisting on the band structure,[144] etc. In 2016, Chang et al. only used STM and STS and discovered robust IP ferroelectricity in atomic-thick SnTe.[108] They utilized STM to observe the domain formation and lattice distortion, and then obtained the clear signatures of band bending via STS. Additionally, polarization switching was also manipulated by applying a voltage pulse between the STM tip and the 1 u.c. SnTe. This discovery opened a new opportunity to explore ferroelectricity in 2D materials, although it is still difficult to observe spontaneous polarization at room temperature at a thickness of
STM提供了一种直接探测局域电子结构的技术，而STS可以提供不同材料电子态密度的信息。STM的原理很简单。它基本上包括在恒定隧道电流下扫描表面上的金属针尖。施加到压电驱动器上的电压引起的导电针尖的位移将产生表面形貌的图像。STM的高分辨率很大程度上取决于隧道电流对两个隧道电极（即金属针尖和扫描表面）之间距离的依赖。[138]STS揭示了零偏压附近强大的电子能隙状特征，以及在能隙边缘以上较高偏压下的显著空间不均匀性。[139,140]事实上，该方法已被用于许多不同的方面，包括表征不均匀的电子结构、[141]原子结构、[139,142]电子-声子/声子-电子相互作用、[143]扭转对能带结构的影响、[144]等。2016年，Chang等人仅使用STM和STS就发现了原子级厚SnTe中强大的面内铁电性。[108]他们利用STM观察畴的形成和晶格畸变，然后通过STS获得能带弯曲的清晰特征。此外，还通过在STM针尖和1个原胞SnTe之间施加电压脉冲来操纵极化反转。这一发现为探索二维材料中的铁电性开辟了新机遇，尽管在室温下薄至
<span class="hljs-strong">**STS**</span>: 扫描隧道谱，基于STM测量电子态密度的谱学技术

1 u. 1 μc. Because of the high requirement and difficulties of STM measurements, no other STM results proving 2D ferroelectricity have been reported since.
1个原胞厚度时仍难以观察到自发极化。由于STM测量要求高且难度大，此后没有其他STM结果证明二维铁电性的报道。

<span class="hljs-section">### 3.2.2. PFM</span>
压电响应力显微镜

PFM based on the converse piezoelectric effect was first demonstrated by Güthner and Dransfeld in 1992 by investigating a ferroelectric polymer film, where they imaged the generated domain pattern by locally poled domains with a tip.[145] In conventional PFM, the domain structure is mapped by scanning the electromechanical response caused by sample surface deformation with a conductive AFM tip, while a small alternating current electric bias is applied to monitor the piezoelectric strain. The results of the amplitude and phase signals reflect the absolute magnitude of the local piezoelectric response and the direction of the ferroelectric polarization.[146] The high-resolution imaging, nanoscale manipulation, and local measurements make PFM an important tool in nanoferroics studies. The exploration of new emergent phenomena, including functional domain walls,[147] tunneling electroresistance (TER),[17,148] flexoelectricity,[88] photovoltaics,[149] topological states,[150] etc.,[151] make PFM a valuable platform for conducting fundamental issues and emergent electronic properties and nanostructures, indicating that PFM is not just a simple technique limited in domain patterning. In addition, a series of review articles regarding PFM can be found, which offer us different outlook for future trends and developments in PFM.[136,152–154] There are typically three typical PFM modes: vector PFM, switching spectroscopy PFM (SS-PFM), and lithography.[153]
基于逆压电效应的PFM由Güthner和Dransfeld于1992年首次展示，他们通过研究铁电聚合物薄膜，利用针尖局部极化畴对产生的畴图案进行成像。[145]在传统PFM中，通过使用导电AFM针尖扫描由样品表面形变引起的机电响应来绘制畴结构图，同时施加小的交流电偏压以监测压电应变。振幅和相位信号的结果反映了局部压电响应的绝对大小和铁电极化的方向。[146]高分辨率成像、纳米级操纵和局域测量使PFM成为纳米铁性研究中的重要工具。对新涌现现象的探索，包括功能畴壁、[147]隧道电致电阻（TER）、[17,148]挠曲电性、[88]光伏、[149]拓扑态、[150]等，[151]使PFM成为开展基础问题和新兴电子性质及纳米结构研究的宝贵平台，表明PFM不仅仅局限于畴图案化的简单技术。此外，可以找到一系列关于PFM的综述文章，为我们提供了PFM未来趋势和发展的不同展望。[136,152–154]通常有三种典型的PFM模式：矢量PFM、开关谱PFM（SS-PFM）和光刻。[153]
<span class="hljs-strong">**Tunneling electroresistance (TER)**</span>: 隧道电致电阻，铁电隧道结中因极化反转引起的电阻变化

1) Vector PFM: In vector PFM, the real-space reconstruction of polarization orientation comes from three components of the piezoresponse: vertical PFM plus at least two orthogonal lateral PFMs. It is performed by imaging both the OOP and IP PFM and enables discrimination between different ferroelectric domains.[155] 2) SS-PFM: PFM spectroscopy refers to locally generated butterfly-like amplitude loops and the sharp phase hysteresis loops in ferroelectric materials. From these hysteresis loops, information on local ferroelectric behavior, such as imprint, local work of switching, and nucleation biases, can be obtained.[156] SS-PFM demonstrates real-space imaging of the energy distribution of nucleation centers in ferroelectrics, thus resolving the structural origins of the Landauer paradox. 3) Lithography: For ferroelectric applications, PFMs can be used to modify the ferroelectric polarization of the sample through the application of an electric field. Switchable polarization can be obtained if the applied electric field is larger than the coercive field. This technique could be used to “write” single domains, domain arrays, and complex patterns without changing the surface topography. With the PFM technique, plenty of fundamental studies based on nanoscale ferroelectric materials, including electric domain patterning,[154] correlation between polarization and electronic transport,[157] etc.,[158] have been conducted.
1) 矢量PFM：在矢量PFM中，极化取向的实空间重建来自三个压电响应分量：垂直PFM加上至少两个正交的横向PFM。它通过对面外和面内PFM进行成像来执行，并能够区分不同的铁电畴。[155] 2) SS-PFM：PFM谱是指铁电材料中局部产生的蝴蝶状振幅回线和尖锐的相位电滞回线。从这些电滞回线中，可以获得局部铁电行为的信息，如印记、局部开关功和成核偏压。[156] SS-PFM展示了铁电体中成核中心能量分布的实空间成像，从而解决了Landauer悖论的结构起源。3) 光刻：对于铁电应用，PFM可用于通过施加电场来修改样品的铁电极化。如果施加的电场大于矫顽场，则可获得可切换的极化。该技术可用于“写入”单个畴、畴阵列和复杂图案，而不改变表面形貌。利用PFM技术，已经开展了大量基于纳米级铁电材料的基础研究，包括电畴图案化、[154]极化与电子输运之间的关联、[157]等。[158]

In the past several years, the results obtained from PFM are usually reliable in characterizing ferroelectric properties, and it has become a standard for imaging ferroelectric domain patterns, as seen from an impressive increase in the number of PFM-related papers published annually.[153,159] However, in recent years, PFM has faced some challenges where ferroelectric-like hysteresis loops and PFM images were observed in various materials, including paraferroelectrics, transition metal oxide thin films and Li-ion conductive glass ceramic, etc.,[160] which could mistake the analysis and interpretation of PFM data and make PFM results become necessary proofs rather than sufficient evidence. PFM is a tool to detect the electrically modulated mechanical strain, and thus, any results caused by a deformation could make it show ferroelectric or ferroelectriclike phenomena, which is the reason that many PFM results obtained from nonferroelectric materials are similar to those of ferroelectrics. In fact, there are several factors, including the electrochemical response,[161] static electric effect,[162] environmental influence,[163] charge injection,[152] etc.,[164,165] that are attributed to these ferroelectric-like PFM responses. Some researchers have tried to offer some approaches to differentiate ferroelectric and nonferroelectric results, indicating that PFM could be applied to study ferroelectrics and could also be used in a broader nonferroelectric field.[165] Therefore, PFM not only has developed as a versatile, easyto-handle, nondestructive tool in studying the fundamental issues and emergent electronic properties but also as a potential manner to probe the local electromechanics of nonferroelectric systems. Since signals of 2D materials are difficult to observe, PFM is a proper candidate for detecting weak signals. In fact, in most recent studies on 2D ferroelectricity, PFM was chosen as the versatile tool. In combination with other techniques, including conducting AFM, magnetic force microscopy, transmission electron microscopy (TEM), etc., identifying 2D ferroelectricity by using PFM makes things much easier and more promising.
在过去的几年中，PFM获得的结果在表征铁电性质方面通常是可靠的，并且它已成为成像铁电畴图案的标准，这从每年发表的PFM相关论文数量显著增加便可见一斑。[153,159]然而，近年来，PFM面临一些挑战，在包括顺电相、过渡金属氧化物薄膜和锂离子导电玻璃陶瓷等各种材料中观察到了类铁电电滞回线和PFM图像，[160]这可能会误导PFM数据的分析和解释，并使PFM结果成为必要证据而非充分证据。PFM是一种检测电调制机械应变的工具，因此，任何由形变引起的结果都可能使其显示铁电或类铁电现象，这就是许多从非铁电材料获得的PFM结果与铁电体相似的原因。事实上，有几个因素，包括电化学响应、[161]静电效应、[162]环境影响、[163]电荷注入、[152]等，[164,165]被归因于这些类铁电PFM响应。一些研究人员试图提供一些方法来区分铁电和非铁电结果，表明PFM不仅可用于研究铁电体，也可用于更广泛的非铁电领域。[165]因此，PFM不仅已发展成为一种多功能、易操作、无损的工具，用于研究基础问题和新兴电子性质，而且也是一种探测非铁电系统局域机电特性的潜在手段。由于二维材料的信号难以观察，PFM是检测弱信号的合适候选者。事实上，在最近的二维铁电性研究中，PFM常被选作多功能工具。结合其他技术，包括导电AFM、磁力显微镜、透射电子显微镜（TEM）等，使用PFM识别二维铁电性使得事情变得更容易、更有前景。

<span class="hljs-section">### 3.2.3. SHG</span>
二次谐波产生

Optical SHG has shown to be a rather simple, nondestructive (electrode-free), and inherently high spatial, spectral and temporal resolutions probe to study the structure, symmetry, and morphology of interfaces and ultrathin films.[134,166] It is a nonlinear optical process based on the principle where a secondorder nonlinear laser-excited coherent optical process involves two photons with the same frequency interacting with a nonlinear material and generating a new photon with twice the energy of the initial photons. SHG was first demonstrated by Franken et al. in 1961[167] and was considered and formulated by Bloembergen and Pershan in 1962.[168] Because of the nonzero second harmonic coefficient, only noncentrosymmetric structures could emit SHG light, which is known to be a sensitive probe of the breakdown of crystallographic symmetry. The detailed principle of SHG is described in the supplementary materials of the report of Xiao et al.[102] Due to the simplicity, surface specificity, and versatility, SHG has become a unique tool for surface studies and has received much attention. Recently, large SHGs were discovered in a number of 2D materials, such as monolayer/multilayer MoS2, MoSe2, WS2, WSe2, h-BN, GaSe, InSe, and AgInP2S6.[71,82,169] Most recently,
光学SHG已被证明是一种相当简单、无损（无电极）、且固有高空间、光谱和时间分辨率的探针，用于研究界面和超薄膜的结构、对称性和形貌。[134,166]它是一种非线性光学过程，基于二阶非线性激光激发相干光学过程涉及两个具有相同频率的光子与非线性材料相互作用，并产生一个能量为初始光子两倍的新光子。SHG由Franken等人在1961年首次演示，[167]并由Bloembergen和Pershan在1962年考虑和公式化。[168]由于非零二阶谐波系数，只有非中心对称结构才能发射SHG光，这被认为是晶体对称性破缺的敏感探针。SHG的详细原理在Xiao等人报告的补充材料中描述。[102]由于简单、表面特异性和多功能性，SHG已成为表面研究的独特工具，并受到广泛关注。最近，在许多二维材料中发现了大的SHG，如单层/多层MoS2、MoSe2、WS2、WSe2、h-BN、GaSe、InSe和AgInP2S6。[71,82,169]最近，

Liu et al.,[52] Chang et al.,[108] Xiao et al.,[102] and Yuan et al.[120] utilized the SHG technique to explore CuInP2S6, SnTe, In2Se3, and MoTe2, respectively, and proved their ferroelectricity via combination with other methods. The results of SHG are usually used to prove the breaking of centrosymmetry, which is the primary condition if 2D ferroelectricity exists. Thus, SHG is also an excellent approach to discover 2D ferroelectric materials with weak ferroelectric signals and ferroelectricparaelectric phase transitions (from a centrosymmetric to a noncentrosymmetric type).
Liu等人、[52]Chang等人、[108]Xiao等人、[102]和Yuan等人[120]利用SHG技术分别探索了CuInP2S6、SnTe、In2Se3和MoTe2，并通过与其他方法结合证明了它们的铁电性。SHG的结果通常用于证明中心对称性的破缺，这是二维铁电性存在的首要条件。因此，SHG也是发现具有弱铁电信号和铁电-顺电相变（从中心对称到非中心对称类型）的二维铁电材料的绝佳方法。

<span class="hljs-section">### 3.2.4. TEM</span>
透射电子显微镜

TEM is a technique in which such features as the crystal structure, defects, etc., could be observed with high spatial resolution via the interactions between the high energy beam of electrons and the atoms of a very thin sample. The technique mainly covers three aspects, including imaging, diffraction, and spectroscopy. For imaging, a row of atoms/lattice as well as defects, including grain boundaries, twins, dislocations, stacking faults, etc., could be seen from HRTEM, while the atomic structure could be obtained from aberration-corrected scanning TEM (AC-TEM). There are usually two imaging modes that could be used in TEM, including the bright field mode where only the transmitted electrons can pass the objective aperture, and the dark field mode where only one diffracted beam is allowed to pass the objective aperture. For diffraction, the selected area electron diffraction (SAED) could give information about the crystal structure of the specimens with high spatial resolution and is usually used to differentiate the different phases. Take, for example, the report in 2018 by Cui et al., who utilized SAED to confirm the existence of ferroelectricity in α-In2Se3 in two flakes with different thickness. The results show that α-In2Se3 ferroelectricity was only observed in the monolayer flake, which is agreeable with the theoretical prediction.[97] For spectroscopy, energy dispersive spectrometer (EDS) and electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) are one of the great advantages of TEM. We could obtain a quantitative X-ray microanalysis, that is, elements valence states from EDS and light elements analysis such as chemical quantitative mapping with high spatial resolution from EELS. Due to the weak vertical ferroelectric signals in the 2D limit, topography changes and complex ferroelectric-like behaviors, PFM alone usually can offer necessary but insufficient evidence to prove 2D ferroelectricity. It is hard to confirm the 2D ferroelectricity through only hysteresis loops and poled phase contrast if the domain structure cannot be observed clearly. In this case, TEM usually plays an important role to make things clear. HRTEM could be used to observe the phases/phase transition[120] while the atomic structure of a specimen could be observed via atomic resolution STEM. In 2018, Xue et al. utilized high-resolution STEM and illustrated that one quintuple layer of In2Se3 consists of the alternately arranged Se-InSe-In-Se atomic planes. Via combination of other techniques, including SHG, PFM, Raman, etc., they showed evidence of OOP and IP piezoelectricity in both the monolayer and multilayer α-In2Se3.[103] In 2019, Yuan et al. utilized HRTEM and AC-TEM to deeply understand the ferroelectric mechanism, which is usually most confusing component of 2D ferroelectricity studies. The trimerized structure of d1T-MoTe2 was confirmed by HRTEM, where the distance between Te and Te atoms is 3.4 Å. The ion displacement mechanism was claimed as a few Te atoms move toward the Mo plane by approximately 0.6 Å in OOP, while the other atoms largely remain still, which has been observed by AC-STEM.[120] In conclusion, studies usually combine different techniques to study the 2D ferroelectricity due to its challenging nature. SHG is usually used to justify the centrosymmetry, while PFM offers evidence of ferroelectricity. To further understand intrinsic mechanisms, atomic structures are usually shown to prove the right specimens’ phases and possible ferroelectric mechanisms, including ion displacement, interlayer translation, etc.
TEM是一种通过高能电子束与非常薄样品原子之间的相互作用，以高空间分辨率观察晶体结构、缺陷等特征的技术。该技术主要涵盖三个方面，包括成像、衍射和谱学。对于成像，从HRTEM中可以看到一排原子/晶格以及缺陷，包括晶界、孪晶、位错、堆垛层错等，而原子结构可通过像差校正扫描TEM（AC-TEM）获得。TEM中通常有两种成像模式，包括明场模式（仅允许透射电子通过物镜光阑）和暗场模式（仅允许一个衍射束通过物镜光阑）。对于衍射，选区电子衍射（SAED）可以提供具有高空间分辨率的样品晶体结构信息，通常用于区分不同的相。例如，2018年Cui等人的报告，他们利用SAED确认了两种不同厚度薄片中α-In2Se3铁电性的存在。结果表明，α-In2Se3铁电性仅在单层薄片中观察到，这与理论预测一致。[97]对于谱学，能量色散谱仪（EDS）和电子能量损失谱（EELS）是TEM的一大优势。我们可以从EDS获得定量X射线微分析，即元素价态，并从EELS获得轻元素分析，如高空间分辨率的化学定量图。由于二维极限下垂直铁电信号微弱、形貌变化和复杂的类铁电行为，单独的PFM通常只能提供必要但不充分的证据来证明二维铁电性。如果无法清晰观察到畴结构，仅通过电滞回线和极化相位对比很难确认二维铁电性。在这种情况下，TEM通常扮演重要角色以澄清问题。HRTEM可用于观察相/相变，[120]而样品的原子结构可通过原子分辨率STEM观察。2018年，Xue等人利用高分辨率STEM，并说明In2Se3的一个五层由交替排列的Se-In-Se-In-Se原子平面组成。通过结合其他技术，包括SHG、PFM、拉曼等，他们展示了单层和多层α-In2Se3中面外和面内压电性的证据。[103]2019年，Yuan等人利用HRTEM和AC-TEM深入理解铁电机制，这通常是二维铁电性研究中最令人困惑的部分。HRTEM确认了d1T-MoTe2的三聚化结构，其中Te与Te原子之间的距离为3.4 Å。离子位移机制被认为是少数Te原子在面外方向向Mo平面移动约0.6 Å，而其他原子基本保持不动，这已通过AC-STEM观察到。[120]总之，由于二维铁电性的挑战性，研究通常结合不同技术来研究。SHG通常用于证明中心对称性，而PFM提供铁电性证据。为了进一步理解内在机制，通常展示原子结构以证明正确的样品相和可能的铁电机制，包括离子位移、层间平移等。
<span class="hljs-strong">**SAED**</span>: 选区电子衍射，一种在TEM中分析晶体结构的技术

<span class="hljs-section"># 4. Intrinsic Mechanisms of 2D Ferroelectricity</span>
二维铁电性的内在机制

Ferroelectric materials are typified as the polar system with electrically switchable macroscopic polarization, arising from spontaneous alignment of electric dipoles. Generally, the emergence of ferroelectric polarization requires an asymmetric structure, and the mechanisms within ferroelectric materials can be divided into two types: ion displacement caused by opposite moving positive and negative charged ions, and electronic polarization induced by asymmetric spin exchange interactions, as shown in Figure 13a.[170] In fact, the forming of net electric dipole is always the key factor for achieving ferroelectricity in 2D materials. Various mechanisms have been reported to date since the pathways used to induce or produce the electric dipole are different. Additionally, other mechanisms, which do not result from polar distortion, have been proposed to find novel approaches to obtain 2D ferroelectrics. Meanwhile, nonintrinsic ferroelectricity induced by artificial designation of 2D materials also makes things more interesting. Based on the various descriptions, we attempt to summarize two major mechanisms of ferroelectricity in 2D materials to simplify the entire story: intralayer bonding and interlayer interactions.
铁电材料是典型的极性系统，具有可电切换的宏观极化，源于电偶极子的自发排列。通常，铁电极化的出现需要不对称结构，铁电材料中的机制可分为两种类型：由相反移动的正负电荷离子引起的离子位移，以及由不对称自旋交换相互作用引起的电子极化，如图13a所示。[170]事实上，形成净电偶极子始终是在二维材料中实现铁电性的关键因素。由于用于诱导或产生电偶极子的途径不同，迄今已报道了各种机制。此外，还提出了其他不源于极性畸变的机制，以寻找获得二维铁电体的新方法。同时，通过人工设计二维材料引起的非本征铁电性也使事情更加有趣。基于各种描述，我们试图总结二维材料中铁电性的两个主要机制以简化整个故事：层内键合和层间相互作用。

<span class="hljs-section">## 4.1. Intralayer Bonding</span>
层内键合

<span class="hljs-section">### 4.1.1. Structure Distortion</span>
结构畸变

For ion displacement ferroelectrics, positive and negative charged ions move in opposite directions, namely, an intrinsic structural distortion as shown in Figure 13a. Among different illustrations, they could be displayed as the displacements of protons,[58] unequal lattice constants and relative atomic displacement,[109] distorted phosphorene lattice structure,[57] surface vacancy-induced distortion,[171] etc. However, despite the various explanations, what essentially remains the same is the occurrence of the dipole moments. Take the report of Chang et al.,[108] for example, who attributed the slightly distorted lattice (from a perfect square to a parallelogram) to the stable IP spontaneous polarization in atomic-thick SnTe by giving evidence of STM, as shown in Figure 9. The parallelogram is elongated along the [110] and its equivalent orientations, and thus the elongated diagonals for two adjacent domains are perpendicular to one another. Similar to the SnTe, SHG-proved 2D ferroelectricity in CuInP2S6 also originates from the distortion of the structure.[52] The nonzero SHG reveals the broken inversion symmetry and ferroelectric order, which is demonstrated in detail in Ref. [52], which reflect the hexagonal ordering of the displaced Cu and In sublattices in the ferroelectric phase. Beyond this, noncentrosymmetric R3m symmetry of the α-In2Se3 was also confirmed experimentally via STEM and SHG. Se atoms move toward the neighboring In atoms as observed by STEM, breaking the inversion symmetry of each quintuple layer and indicating the origin of ferroelectricity in α-In2Se3.[101] In fact, ion displacement is a popular explanation for ferroelectricity in traditional 3D ferroelectric materials. For 2D ferroelectrics, the mechanism also plays an important role in studying or designing ferroelectric polarization. The occurrence of the electric dipole is usually the mark of ferroelectricity. So far, many distorted ferroelectric structures are predicted in theory, mostly based on DFT calculations, including a d1TMoS2,[45] group IV monochalcogenides,[69,70,74] decorated phosphorene,[73] phosphorene-like structures,[172] etc. With the rapid development of 2D ferroelectrics, today an increasing number of structure-distortion induced 2D ferroelectricity have been discovered and proven by experimental methods, which has made the intrinsic mechanism in 2D ferroelectrics clearer.
对于离子位移型铁电体，正负电荷离子沿相反方向移动，即如图13a所示的本征结构畸变。在不同的说明中，它们可表现为质子位移、[58]不等晶格常数和相对原子位移、[109]畸变的磷烯晶格结构、[57]表面空位诱导的畸变、[171]等。然而，尽管解释多样，本质上保持不变的是偶极矩的产生。以Chang等人的报告为例，[108]他们通过提供STM证据，将轻微畸变的晶格（从完美正方形到平行四边形）归因于原子级厚SnTe中稳定的面内自发极化，如图9所示。平行四边形沿[110]及其等效方向伸长，因此两个相邻畴的伸长对角线相互垂直。与SnTe类似，SHG证明的CuInP2S6二维铁电性也源于结构畸变。[52]非零SHG揭示了反转对称性破缺和铁电有序，这在文献[52]中有详细展示，反映了铁电相中位移的Cu和In亚晶格的六方有序化。除此之外，α-In2Se3的非中心对称R3m对称性也通过STEM和SHG实验证实。如STEM观察到的，Se原子向邻近的In原子移动，打破了每个五层的反转对称性，并指示了α-In2Se3中铁电性的起源。[101]事实上，离子位移是传统三维铁电材料中铁电性的流行解释。对于二维铁电体，该机制在研究或设计铁电极化方面也发挥着重要作用。电偶极子的出现通常是铁电性的标志。迄今为止，许多畸变铁电结构在理论上被预测，主要基于DFT计算，包括d1T-MoS2、[45]IV族单硫族化物、[69,70,74]修饰的磷烯、[73]类磷烯结构、[172]等。随着二维铁电体的快速发展，如今越来越多的结构畸变诱导的二维铁电性已被实验方法发现并证明，这使二维铁电体中的内在机制更加清晰。

<span class="hljs-section">### 4.1.2. Dipole Locking/Covalent Bond Configuration</span>
偶极锁定/共价键构型

2D ferroelectric materials with dipole locking are based on the unique covalent bond reconstruction (Figure 13b). Distinct from conventional ferroelectric switching, where small uniaxial atomic distortions occur without covalent bond breaking and forming, the breaking of old covalent bonds and forming of new bonds is the mark of this mechanism. The intrinsically intercorrelated OOP and IP polarization against the depolarization field opens up new approaches to explore 2D ferroelectric physics. As mentioned in Section 2.2.2, Xiao et al. demonstrated the mechanism of dipole locking, leading to the observation of intrinsic 2D OOP ferroelectricity in atomically thin In2Se3 crystals with thicknesses down to 3 nm.[102] They found the switching of vertical electric field could control IP lattice orientation with the middle Se atom moving laterally and In-Se covalent bonds breaking and forming. Additionally, Cui et al. also attributed the dipole effect, where the reversal of the OOP polarization caused by the vertical electric field induces the rotation of the IP polarization to the ferroelectricity in 2D-layered semiconductor In2Se3.[97] However, the mechanism in In2Se3 described by Ding et al. is attributed to the polar displacement rather than the dipole coupling, which means the mechanisms in In2Se3 is no longer simple.[48] Similar to the interactions between OOP and IP polarization, ferroelectric and ferromagnetism coupling might appear in 2D multiferroics with the effect of covalent bonds reconstruction, which could be designed for incorporating into circuits. In 2017, Yang et al. showed that covalent-bonded halogen-decorated phosphorene systems could achieve electric writing and magnetic reading with robust OOP polarization against depolarization field.[73] In this case, unique covalent bonds configuration in 2D materials may become a potential pathway to break the barrier in orders coupling, especially for multiferroics.
具有偶极锁定的二维铁电材料基于独特的共价键重建（图13b）。不同于传统铁电切换（发生小的单轴原子畸变而不涉及共价键的断裂和形成），旧共价键的断裂和新键的形成是这一机制的标志。本质上面内和面外极化相互关联以对抗退极化场，为探索二维铁电物理开辟了新途径。正如第2.2.2节所述，Xiao等人展示了偶极锁定机制，导致在原子级薄In2Se3晶体中观察到薄至3 nm的本征二维面外铁电性。[102]他们发现垂直电场的切换可以控制面内晶格取向，中间Se原子横向移动，In-Se共价键断裂和形成。此外，Cui等人也将偶极效应（由垂直电场引起的面外极化反转导致面内极化旋转）归因于二维层状半导体In2Se3中的铁电性。[97]然而，Ding等人描述的In2Se3中的机制被归因于极性位移而非偶极耦合，这意味着In2Se3中的机制不再简单。[48]类似于面外和面内极化之间的相互作用，在共价键重建的作用下，铁电性和铁磁性的耦合可能出现在二维多铁材料中，并可设计用于集成到电路中。2017年，Yang等人展示了共价键合的卤素修饰磷烯系统可以实现电写入和磁读取，具有强大的面外极化以对抗退极化场。[73]在这种情况下，二维材料中独特的共价键构型可能成为打破有序耦合障碍的潜在途径，特别是对于多铁材料。

<span class="hljs-section">### 4.1.3. Electronic Polarization/Spin Exchange Interactions</span>
电子极化/自旋交换相互作用

For electronic polarization, the net electric dipole is induced by the asymmetric spin exchange interactions, while the dispersion of electrons in 2D materials could be manipulated by the external electric field, as shown in Figure 13a. Hu et al. predicted that IP atomic-thick ferroelectricity could be induced by the vertical electric field in both monolayer and bilayer n-A-PNRs (n: label the width; A-PNR: armchair phosphorene nanoribbons) where n = odd number.[170] Take a 9-A-PNR as an example: the differential charge density, the significant charge accumulation on the P−P bonds connecting different halflayers and the reduced charge on P−P bonds connecting the same half-layer demonstrate the significant charge redistribution along the ribbon direction, leading to the emergence of polarization. It is perpendicular to the direction of the external field and lies in the plane. This report first shows the IP ferroelectricity with the electron polarization induced by spin ordering, which may motivate new exciting results and applications. Huang et al. revealed multiferroics in charged CrBr3 monolayer nanostructures combined with a large electric dipole and ferromagnetic ordering.[173] In these systems, the IP ferroelectricity is ensured by the combination of the charge order and orbital order, which are induced by the asymmetric Jahn–Teller distortions of the two neighboring Cr-Br6 units.
对于电子极化，净电偶极子由不对称自旋交换相互作用诱导，而二维材料中电子的色散可由外电场操纵，如图13a所示。Hu等人预测，在n为奇数的单层和双层n-A-PNRs（n：标记宽度；A-PNR：扶手椅型磷烯纳米带）中，垂直电场可诱导原子级厚度的面内铁电性。[170]以9-A-PNR为例：差分电荷密度显示，连接不同半层的P−P键上显著的电荷积累，以及连接同一半层的P−P键上电荷减少，表明沿纳米带方向发生了显著的电荷重新分布，导致极化的出现。它垂直于外场方向并位于平面内。该报告首次展示了由自旋有序诱导的电子极化引起的面内铁电性，这可能会激发新的令人兴奋的结果和应用。Huang等人揭示了在带电CrBr3单层纳米结构中结合大电偶极子和铁磁有序的多铁性。[173]在这些体系中，面内铁电性由电荷有序和轨道有序的组合保证，这是由两个相邻Cr-Br6单元的不对称Jahn–Teller畸变引起的。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Jahn–Teller distortion**</span>: 杨-泰勒畸变，非线性分子中电子简并引起的几何畸变

<span class="hljs-section">## 4.2. Interlayer Translation</span>
层间平移

The interlayer translation could change the interlayer potential and drive the flow of electrons, which is based on bilayer or multilayer 2D materials. In 2017, Li et al. reported that 2D vertical ferroelectricity existing in the graphitic bilayer of BN, AlN, ZnO, MoS2, GaSe, etc. is induced by the interlayer translation, where a closer interlayer distance may lead to a stronger charge transfer from the upper layer to the down layer.[49] As shown in Figure 13c, the polarization could be switched through an interlayer transition by one bond length, indicating a translation pathway between the upper and down layers. The origin of 2D ferroelectricity in this case is similar to the analysis in WTe2, which gives more possibilities for discovering and designing 2D ferroelectrics.[174] Interestingly, in the experimental report published in 2018, the origin of the vertical polarization in metal ferroelectric WTe2 is attributed to the electron-hole correlation rather than a lattice instability. They discussed three reasons that make electron-hole correlation effects more likely to be the mechanism of the polarization in WTe2: monolayer conductance at 4 K is almost independent of the vertical electric field; and the polarization flips when the applied potential difference exceeds the spontaneous polarization induced potential and the compensation point at which the electron and hole densities are exactly equal.[115] In addition to these, Ding et al. also demonstrated that the intrinsic 2D ferroelectricity in In2Se3 is induced by different interlayer spacing between the central Se and two In layers (Figure 2b).[48] Later in 2018, the origin of 2H In2Se3 was also attributed to the inequivalent interlayer spacing between the Se atom layer and the adjacent two In atom layers.[106] In addition, the polarization and the interlayer voltage could be enhanced if the interlayer distance is further decreased upon vertical pressure. In the same year, Wang et al. predicted a new kind of 2D ferroelectricity in elemental tellurium multilayers, which exhibits spontaneous IP polarization due to the interlayer interaction between lone pairs.[56] Additionally, the ferroelectric Moire superlattice could also be obtained by twisting a small angle between the layers, where a slight difference in strain indicates the generation of different domains.[174] From these excellent works, we can obtain a new vision of studying 2D ferroelectrics: focusing instead on monolayer, bilayer, or multilayer 2D materials, which could be ideal candidates for applications in spintronics and electronics at the nanoscale. Meanwhile, the effect of the even-odd number of layers should also be taken into the consideration since the breaking of inversion symmetry can sometimes only exist in odd-numbered layers.[170,175] In conclusion, the key to the ferroelectricity in 2D materials is the absence of inversion symmetry, regardless of what mechanism causes it. There are three methods to classifying 2D ferroelectric mechanisms: 1) according to the position where the dipole moment is formed, it could be divided into intralayer and interlayer interactions; 2) if different sorts of particles, including ions and electrons, are taken into consideration, intrinsic structural distortion and asymmetric electronic redistribution (electron polarization) could be the intrinsic mechanisms of 2D ferroelectricity; and 3) when the bonds configuration are different, ion displacement and covalent bonding might be the reason for the occurrence of ferroelectricity in 2D materials. On the basis of these mechanisms, a few other explanations are also discussed in some materials, including hyperferroelectric metals,[114] 2D perovskite oxide thin films,[122] etc. Additionally, many methods, including importing vacancies,[171] applying strain,[75] changing displacement of adjacent layers or atomics,[66,109] etc., have also been used to fabricate or discover the 2D ferroelectrics. At the same time, organic materials are playing an important role in 2D ferroelectricity as well, where multimode proton transfer,[67] molecule rotation,[176] etc., could be the intrinsic mechanisms within them. Although ferroelectric behaviors and the corresponding mechanisms have been studied in 2D materials and proven by some methods, including SHG, TEM, PFM, etc., it is still controversial and ambiguous in different material systems, which still has a large space for further exploration.
层间平移可以改变层间电势并驱动电子流动，这基于双层或多层二维材料。2017年，Li等人报道了石墨相双层BN、AlN、ZnO、MoS2、GaSe等中存在的二维垂直铁电性是由层间平移引起的，其中更近的层间距离可能导致从上层到下层的更强电荷转移。[49]如图13c所示，极化可通过一个键长的层间转变进行切换，表明上层和下层之间的平移路径。在这种情况下，二维铁电性的起源与WTe2中的分析类似，这为发现和设计二维铁电体提供了更多可能性。[174]有趣的是，在2018年发表的实验报告中，金属铁电体WTe2中垂直极化的起源被归因于电子-空穴关联而非晶格不稳定性。他们讨论了三个原因，使电子-空穴关联效应更可能是WTe2中极化的机制：4 K时单层电导几乎与垂直电场无关；当施加的电位差超过自发极化诱导的电位以及电子和空穴密度精确相等的补偿点时，极化翻转。[115]除此之外，Ding等人还证明了In2Se3中的本征二维铁电性是由中心Se和两个In层之间不同的层间距引起的（图2b）。[48]随后在2018年，2H In2Se3的起源也被归因于Se原子层与相邻两个In原子层之间不等价的层间距。[106]此外，如果层间距在垂直压力下进一步减小，极化和层间电压可能会增强。同年，Wang等人预测了元素碲多层膜中的一种新型二维铁电性，由于孤对电子之间的层间相互作用而表现出自发面内极化。[56]此外，通过层间扭转一个小角度也可以获得铁电莫尔超晶格，其中应变的微小差异表明不同畴的产生。[174]从这些出色的工作中，我们可以获得研究二维铁电体的新视角：转而关注单层、双层或多层二维材料，它们可能是纳米尺度自旋电子学和电子学应用的理想候选者。同时，还应考虑奇偶层数的影响，因为反转对称性的破缺有时仅存在于奇数层中。[170,175]总之，二维材料中铁电性的关键是反转对称性的缺失，无论是什么机制导致。有三种方法可对二维铁电机制进行分类：1) 根据偶极矩形成的位置，可分为层内和层间相互作用；2) 如果考虑不同种类的粒子，包括离子和电子，本征结构畸变和不对称电子重新分布（电子极化）可能是二维铁电性的内在机制；3) 当键合构型不同时，离子位移和共价键合可能是二维材料中铁电性产生的原因。在这些机制的基础上，一些其他解释也在一些材料中进行了讨论，包括超铁电金属、[114]二维钙钛矿氧化物薄膜、[122]等。此外，许多方法，包括引入空位、[171]施加应变、[75]改变相邻层或原子的位移、[66,109]等，也被用于制备或发现二维铁电体。同时，有机材料在二维铁电性中也发挥着重要作用，其中多模式质子转移、[67]分子旋转、[176]等可能是其内在机制。尽管铁电行为及其相应机制已在二维材料中进行研究，并通过SHG、TEM、PFM等方法得到证明，但在不同的材料体系中仍存在争议和模糊之处，仍有很大的探索空间。

<span class="hljs-section"># 5. Applications and Challenges of 2D Ferroelectric Materials</span>
二维铁电材料的应用与挑战

Due to the limitations of intrinsic ferroelectric mechanisms and the few experimental observations in 2D ferroelectric materials, extensive applications are naturally rare. Therefore, based on the conventional development of ferroelectric materials, applications and outlooks of 2D ferroelectrics and the related work on 2D materials and traditional ferroelectrics are briefly introduced here.
由于内在铁电机制的局限性和二维铁电材料中实验观察的稀少，广泛的应用自然很少。因此，基于铁电材料的常规发展，这里简要介绍二维铁电体的应用和展望，以及二维材料和传统铁电体的相关工作。

<span class="hljs-section">## 5.1. Applications of 2D Ferroelectric Materials</span>
二维铁电材料的应用

<span class="hljs-section">### 5.1.1. Devices Based on 2D Ferroelectric Materials</span>
基于二维铁电材料的器件

Compared with conventional FeRAM, where reading is destructive and unstable at room temperature when the thickness is down to a few unit cells, the memory based on the ultrathin planar morphology of 2D ferroelectric materials may be nondestructive and more reproducible, which allows for the possibility of electrical modulation of functional properties of the hybrid ferroelectric memory[2,177] and logic device applications, including FeFET,[3,178,179] ferroelectric junctions, and multiferroic junctions (FTJs),[17,60,180] ferroelectric capacitor,[5,181] switchable diode,[182] etc. At the same time, 2D ferroelectric materials also have additional advantages over traditional nonvolatile memory, such as a band tuning ability, the miniature size in the OOP direction and the moderated substrate requirement condition.[42] FTJs consist of two normal metal electrodes with a ferroelectric tunnel barrier, which is popular as the sandwich structure. It is known that the reversal of the polarization would change the potential barrier, which is denoted as the TER effect. The performance of FTJs is sensitive to the electrical boundary conditions and could be controlled by electrode materials and interface engineering. Taking some theoretical works for example, as shown in Figure 14a, ferroelectric junctions of germanene passivated by –CH2F and –CH3, IP heterostructure of germanene passivated by –CH2F and –I and ferroelectric PN junction based on a hydroxylized Si (111) surface and MoS2 on a hydroxylized silica surface were designed.[60] All of these devices take advantage of the combination of a high-mobility semiconductor with fast writing and nondestructive reading in nonvolatile memories. In 2018, a traditional ferroelectric material, tetragonal BFO ultrathin film, in the 2D limit was demonstrated at room temperature. By utilizing PFM, Kelvin probe force microscopy, aberration-corrected STEM, and energy dispersive X-ray, stable ferroelectricity was observed in few unit cells, including 1 u.c. thick BFO films. When the 1 u.c. BFO barrier layer was used in a FTJ device, a large ON/OFF ratio of 370% was obtained, while a much larger TER ratio of 2700% was achieved in a 2 u.c. thick BFO FTJ, which make ultrathin BFO films in the 2D limit promising candidates for high-density data storage.[18] Additionally, the devices consisting of 2D materials, such as a graphitic bilayer of BN and some 2D ferroelectrics, could be utilized as nanogenerators, piezotronics, and pressurizing FET.[49,66] The observed tunable band alignments with the ferroelectric layer make them promising devices as nonvolatile
与传统FeRAM相比，当厚度降至几个原胞时，读取在室温下具有破坏性且不稳定，而基于二维铁电材料超薄平面形态的存储器可能是非破坏性的且更具可重复性，这使得对混合铁电存储器的功能特性进行电调制成为可能，[2,177]以及逻辑器件应用，包括FeFET、[3,178,179]铁电结和多铁结（FTJs）、[17,60,180]铁电电容器、[5,181]可切换二极管、[182]等。同时，二维铁电材料与传统非易失性存储器相比还具有额外优势，如能带可调性、面外方向的微型尺寸以及适中的衬底要求条件。[42]FTJs由两个正常金属电极和铁电隧道势垒组成，广泛采用三明治结构。众所周知，极化反转会改变势垒，这被称为TER效应。FTJs的性能对电学边界条件敏感，并可通过电极材料和界面工程进行控制。以一些理论工作为例，如图14a所示，设计了由–CH2F和–CH3钝化的锗烯铁电结、由–CH2F和–I钝化的锗烯面内异质结构以及基于羟基化Si（111）表面和羟基化二氧化硅表面上的MoS2的铁电PN结。[60]所有这些器件都利用了高迁移率半导体与非易失性存储器中快速写入和非破坏性读取的结合优势。2018年，在室温下展示了二维极限下的传统铁电材料四方相BFO超薄膜。通过利用PFM、开尔文探针力显微镜、像差校正STEM和能量色散X射线，在包括1个原胞厚BFO薄膜的少数原胞中观察到了稳定的铁电性。当1个原胞BFO势垒层用于FTJ器件时，获得了370%的大开/关比，而在2个原胞厚的BFO FTJ中实现了2700%的更大TER比，这使得二维极限下的超薄BFO薄膜成为高密度数据存储的有前景的候选者。[18]此外，由二维材料（如石墨相双层BN和一些二维铁电体）组成的器件可用作纳米发电机、压电电子学和加压FET。[49,66]观察到的与铁电层可调能带排列使它们成为有前景的非易失性

memory- and graphene-based electronics. Additionally, bilayer heterostructure side views and band structures formed by stacking ferroelectric In2Se3 on nonferroelectric semiconductor WSe2 and graphene are shown in Figure 14b. The switching of the electric dipole of the In2Se3 layer caused an alternated Schottky barrier across the interface and the significant band gap reduction makes the In2Se3-based heterostructure a promising device.[48] Most recently, Shen et al. proposed a planar p-type semiconductor/ferroelectric/n-type semiconductor 2D FTJs via doping engineering. According to the k||-resolved transmissions calculations shown in Figure 14c, the giant TER effect of 1460% could be obtained by integrating the transmission probability for states at a Fermi energy over the 2D Brillouin zone, which is significantly comparable to the traditional FTJs and is claimed as the result of the accumulation/depletion of major carriers near the semiconductor in response to polarization switching. This is the first demonstration of the 2D FTJ based on homostructures that promise future ultralow-power, high-speed, and nonvolatile nanoscale applications.[183] Experimentally, although only few 2D materials have been proven to be ferroelectrics, they perform well in electric transformation and could potentially be used as light sensors, energy
存储器和基于石墨烯的电子器件。此外，图14b显示了通过将铁电In2Se3堆叠在非铁电半导体WSe2和石墨烯上形成的双层异质结构侧视图和能带结构。In2Se3层电偶极子的切换导致界面处肖特基势垒交替变化，显著的带隙缩减使基于In2Se3的异质结构成为一种有前景的器件。[48]最近，Shen等人通过掺杂工程提出了一种平面p型半导体/铁电体/n型半导体二维FTJs。根据图14c所示的k||分辨透射计算，通过将费米能级处态的透射概率在二维布里渊区积分，可获得1460%的巨大TER效应，这与传统FTJs显著可比，并被声称是半导体附近主要载流子响应极化反转的积累/耗尽的结果。这是首次展示基于同质结构的二维FTJ，有望实现未来超低功耗、高速和非易失性的纳米级应用。[183]实验上，尽管只有少数二维材料被证明为铁电体，但它们在电学转换中表现良好，并可能用于光传感器、能量
<span class="hljs-strong">**Schottky barrier**</span>: 肖特基势垒，金属与半导体接触形成的势垒

conversion devices, and for nonvolatile memory. Figure 15a shows the proposed CuInP2S6 transistors composed of an MoS2 flake and CuInP2S6.[179] The dual-gate structure with a proper back-gate voltage enables an electrically controllable device and the metal-insulator-metal capacitor structure shows resistive switching with more than four orders of ON/OFF ratios. Compared with CuInP2S6, the electric transport of the In2Se3 device could be determined by both OOP and IP polarization through the diode effect, as shown in Figure 15b.[97] In addition, the SnTe memory was also fabricated based on a transistor structure where the voltage between the source and drain is used to modulate the polarization direction, and thus changes the tunneling current between the ferroelectric film and the source electrode (Figure 15c).[108]
转换器件和非易失性存储器。图15a展示了由MoS2薄片和CuInP2S6组成的拟议CuInP2S6晶体管。[179]具有适当背栅电压的双栅结构实现了电可控器件，金属-绝缘体-金属电容器结构显示出超过四个数量级开/关比的电阻切换。与CuInP2S6相比，In2Se3器件的电输运可通过二极管效应由面外和面内极化共同决定，如图15b所示。[97]此外，SnTe存储器也基于晶体管结构制造，其中源漏之间的电压用于调制极化方向，从而改变铁电薄膜与源电极之间的隧道电流（图15c）。[108]

<span class="hljs-section">### 5.1.2. Combination of 2D Materials and 3D Ferroelectric Materials</span>
二维材料与三维铁电材料的结合

Ferroelectric materials with electric modulated spontaneous polarization have potential prospects in sensors,[184] photonics and energy-efficient memories,[185] solar cells,[186] and photoelectrochemical applications.[5,187] In 2017, Wang and Hu published a review about the integration between 2D materials and ferroelectrics, describing the logical and optoelectrical applications.[188] In the same year, Zhou and Chai in detail reviewed studies on 2D electron devices, such as low-power FET, optothermal device, and photoelectronic device.[189] These articles not only describe the progress in integrated 2D devices but also give us a clear skeleton to learn what is happening in this field. Here, we just give a brief introduction of 2D integrated devices. In one type of these devices, for example, FTJs, 2D materials, such as graphene[190] and MoS2,[191] have been used as the top electrode to apply a polarization switching bias, as shown in Figure 16a,b. In the graphene/BTO interface, molecular layers could be easily introduced to the interface, which efficiently stabilizes the polarization of BTO and significantly enhances the TER effect. For the graphene/BTO FTJ with the interfacial ammonia layer, a TER effect of 6% × 105% was obtained, indicating the important implications in nonvolatile memories and logic devices.[190] Compared with graphene as the top electrode, 2H-MoS2 is less conductive. The asymmetric switching behavior indicates the dependence of the n/p-type MoS2 on the polarization direction, and the TER effect with an ON/OFF ratio as high as 104 makes the 2D/3D structure a novel pathway for electronic devices with an enhanced performance.[191] By controlling the OOP tunneling conductance across the ferroelectric layer, unique polarization switching has been obtained. The extraordinary properties and characteristics at the interface could be explored with hybrid structures composed of ferroelectric thin films and functional 2D materials, providing a simple and straightforward method for interface engineering and resistive switching manipulation in FTJs.[192] In another type of 2D ferroelectric devices, FeFETs, 2D materials on a ferroelectric substrate were not only used as a gate dielectric to alter the IP conductivity of the adjacent 2D channel,[193] but also used as the channel gated with a ferroelectric film to obtain a high ON/OFF ratio, a long time retention and a high endurance.[194] Among these applications, for example, optoelectrical ferroelectric memory was obtained by tuning the ON/OFF ratio of the light.[195] As shown in Figure 17a, the MoS2-PZT FeFET exhibits a large hysteresis of electronic transport and the possibility to write and erase both electrically and optically. In addition to this, polarizationdependent hysteresis electronic transport in graphene-PZT FeFET is also demonstrated by Lipatov et al.[196] Additionally, 2D ferroelectric/nonferroelectric junction based on functionalized 2D materials or semiconductor surfaces with proper substitution has been designed to obtain high TER.[48,60] What is shown in Figure 17b is ferroelectric FET with 2D MoSe2 channels. The device consisted of 5 nm MoSe2 and could yield a write/erase ratio of more than 103 with a good retention and endurance performance.[178]
具有电调制自发极化的铁电材料在传感器、[184]光子学和节能存储器、[185]太阳能电池、[186]和光电化学应用等方面具有潜在前景。[5,187]2017年，Wang和Hu发表了一篇关于二维材料与铁电体集成的综述，描述了逻辑和光电应用。[188]同年，Zhou和Chai详细回顾了二维电子器件的研究，如低功耗FET、光热器件和光电子器件。[189]这些文章不仅描述了集成二维器件的进展，还为我们提供了一个清晰的框架，以了解该领域的进展。在此，我们仅简要介绍二维集成器件。其中一类器件，例如FTJs，二维材料如石墨烯[190]和MoS2[191]已用作顶电极以施加极化切换偏压，如图16a,b所示。在石墨烯/BTO界面中，分子层可轻易引入界面，有效稳定BTO的极化并显著增强TER效应。对于具有界面氨层的石墨烯/BTO FTJ，获得了6% × 105%的TER效应，表明在非易失性存储器和逻辑器件中的重要应用。[190]与作为顶电极的石墨烯相比，2H-MoS2导电性较差。不对称切换行为表明n/p型MoS2对极化方向的依赖性，且开/关比高达104的TER效应使二维/三维结构成为增强性能电子器件的一条新途径。[191]通过控制跨越铁电层的面外隧道电导，获得了独特的极化切换。由铁电薄膜和功能二维材料组成的混合结构可探索界面处的非凡性质和特性，为FTJs中的界面工程和电阻切换操纵提供了一种简单直接的方法。[192]在另一类二维铁电器件铁电场效应晶体管（FeFETs）中，铁电衬底上的二维材料不仅用作栅介质以改变相邻二维沟道的面内电导率，[193]还用作铁电薄膜栅控的沟道，以获得高开/关比、长保持时间和高耐久性。[194]在这些应用中，例如，通过调节光的开/关比获得了光电铁电存储器。[195]如图17a所示，MoS2-PZT FeFET展现出大的电输运滞回以及电学和光学写入和擦除的可能性。除此之外，Lipatov等人还展示了石墨烯-PZT FeFET中极化依赖的滞回电子输运。[196]此外，基于功能化二维材料或具有适当取代的半导体表面的二维铁电/非铁电结已被设计以获得高TER。[48,60]图17b所示的是具有二维MoSe2沟道的铁电FET。该器件由5 nm MoSe2组成，可产生超过103的写入/擦除比，并具有良好的保持和耐久性能。[178]

<span class="hljs-section">### 5.1.3. 2D Ferroelectricity-Induced Unique Properties</span>
二维铁电性诱导的独特性质

After discussing the applications of 2D ferroelectricity, ferroelectricity-induced novel properties are listed here. As shown in Figure 18a, the occurrence of a reduced symmetry of the valley shown in the band structure of the ferroelectric phase GeSe monolayer implies that the spontaneous valley polarization is induced by 2D IP ferroelectricity.[197] Additionally, the investigations into the Rashba spin orbit coupling at the interface of a GeTe(111)/InP(111) superlattice indicate that the reversal of the spin texture could be tuned by the ferroelectric polarization, indicating a great potential in ferroelectric Rashba semiconductors spin FET. It can be clearly seen from Figure 18b that with different ferroelectric displacements, the energy levels of the Rashba splitting bands gradually shift up or down.[198] Different from the previous works, the monolayer MoS2 growth and transport properties could also be controlled by the ferroelectric substrate.[199] While maintaining the substrate LiNbO3 polarization pattern, the monolayer MoS2 growth exhibits a preference for the ferroelectric up-domains in the periodic domain stripes, as shown in Figure 18c, indicating the possibility for ferroelectric nonvolatile gating of TMDs in scalable devices without the traditional exfoliation and transferring.
在讨论了二维铁电性的应用之后，这里列出铁电性诱导的新奇性质。如图18a所示，铁电相GeSe单层能带结构中谷的对称性降低意味着自发谷极化是由二维面内铁电性诱导的。[197]此外，对GeTe(111)/InP(111)超晶格界面处Rashba自旋轨道耦合的研究表明，自旋纹理的反转可由铁电极化调控，表明在铁电Rashba半导体自旋FET中具有巨大潜力。从图18b可以清楚地看出，随着不同的铁电位移，Rashba分裂能带的能级逐渐上移或下移。[198]与先前工作不同，单层MoS2的生长和输运性质也可由铁电衬底控制。[199]在保持衬底LiNbO3极化图案的同时，单层MoS2生长在周期性畴条纹中表现出对铁电向上畴的偏好，如图18c所示，表明在无需传统剥离和转移的可扩展器件中，铁电非易失性栅控TMDs的可能性。

<span class="hljs-section"># 6. Opportunities and Outlook</span>
机遇与展望

In the field of 2D ferroelectric materials, despite many 2D materials having been predicted and studied, the exploration of intrinsic 2D ferroelectricity within a plane is still in an early stage, especially experimentally. During the process of developing 2D ferroelectrics, theoretical calculations usually provide directional suggestions to explore and prove 2D ferroelectricity. Although it is still very challenging to observe stable and strong spontaneous polarization in 2D materials with a few unit cells, we will have an increasing amount of approaches to discover novel 2D ferroelectric materials, including the underlying physical properties and intrinsic mechanisms, with the rapid development of characterization and fabrication methods. Since the exploration of new 2D ferroelectric materials is always an attractive topic, new fundamental studies could possibly address the effects of strain, functional dopant, interlayer interactions (bilayers and/or multilayers), intralayer bonding, edge effects in transport, collective defects, oxygen vacancies, multiferroic orders coupling, etc. Additionally, IP and OOP correlation could be efficiently used to explore 2D ferroelectricity, that is, inducing vertical polarization via IP displacement. Meanwhile, the exploration of 2D ferroelectricity will also contribute to new scientific frontiers, such as spintronics and valleytronics, leading to exciting technological advances. Additionally, some freestanding traditional ferroelectrics, such as BiFeO3, down to a single unit cell with robust ferroelectricity could also be fabricated through the unique methods, indicating that the chances to break the limitation of the size effect for traditional ferroelectric materials still exist and the development of traditional ferroelectric materials down to the nanoscale cannot be ignored.[18,200] In addition, the change in topography is an unavoidable problem during 2D ferroelectricity studies, so to overcome and break through this is another key point to obtaining further improvement of this field. Finally, with the increasing number of discoveries of 2D ferroelectric materials to date, minimized and integrated functional electronics are expected soon, which will open a new scientific era.
在二维铁电材料领域，尽管许多二维材料已被预测和研究，但对面内本征二维铁电性的探索仍处于早期阶段，尤其是实验方面。在发展二维铁电体的过程中，理论计算通常为探索和证明二维铁电性提供方向性建议。尽管在具有少数原胞的二维材料中观察稳定且强大的自发极化仍非常具有挑战性，但随着表征和制备方法的快速发展，我们将有越来越多的途径来发现新型二维铁电材料，包括潜在的物理性质和内在机制。由于探索新型二维铁电材料始终是一个有吸引力的主题，新的基础研究可能涉及应变、功能掺杂、层间相互作用（双层和/或多层）、层内键合、输运中的边缘效应、集体缺陷、氧空位、多铁有序耦合等的影响。此外，面内和面外关联可有效用于探索二维铁电性，即通过面内位移诱导垂直极化。同时，二维铁电性的探索也将为自旋电子学和谷电子学等新的科学前沿做出贡献，带来激动人心的技术进步。此外，一些独立式传统铁电体，如BiFeO3，通过独特的方法也可制备出薄至单个原胞且具有强大铁电性的材料，表明打破传统铁电材料尺寸效应限制的机会仍然存在，传统铁电材料向纳米尺度的发展不容忽视。[18,200]此外，形貌的变化是二维铁电性研究中不可避免的问题，因此克服和突破这一点是获得该领域进一步改进的另一个关键点。最后，随着迄今为止发现的二维铁电材料越来越多，微型化和集成化的功能电子学有望很快实现，这将开启一个新的科学时代。
<span class="hljs-strong">**Valleytronics**</span>: 谷电子学，利用能带中谷自由度进行信息处理的技术

## <span style="color: rgb(0, 174, 137);">❹ 🤖️ 太奶阅读法🍈</span>

> <span class="AIReading">🤖 AI 解读，快人一步</span>
> 
> > 我们需要理解用户的需求。用户自称是一位100岁的太奶，对英文和专业术语不熟悉，希望我把这篇关于二维铁电材料的综述文章“说道说道”，让她能彻底看懂。她要求“最好是翻译出来”，并且重点解释专业术语，最后还要总结论文的题目、摘要、研究问题、方法、结论、创新点和不足之处。
> > 
> > 首先，我需要仔细阅读这篇论文的内容。这是一篇发表在《Advanced Electronic Materials》上的综述，标题是“Recent Progress in Two-Dimensional Ferroelectric Materials”。文章很长，包括摘要、引言、多个章节，以及参考文献等。用户提供的文本似乎是从论文中截取的部分，包括摘要、引言的一部分、作者信息等。文章后面还有大量内容，但用户给的是从“1900818 (1 of 30)”开始的几页，包括摘要、引言的一部分，以及一些图表说明。但用户要求我把整篇文章解释清楚，所以我需要基于用户提供的文本来总结。用户给的文本并不完整，只是论文的前几页，但包含了摘要和引言的大部分。我需要基于这些内容进行解释。
> >

> * * *
> 
> ### 一、先把文章整体给您说说
> 
> 这篇文章，好比是一本“寻宝图”，讲的是怎么在比头发丝还细千倍的材料里，找到一种能“记住”电方向的宝贝，并且能把它做得又小又好用。这帮科学家们，想找一种叫“二维铁电材料”的东西。
> 
> -   **二维**：您就想象一张纸，特别特别薄，薄到只有一层原子，那就叫“二维材料”。平得跟画儿似的，没有厚度。
> -   **铁电**：这跟“铁”没啥关系，是说这材料能像个小电池，自己带着正负极（这叫“自发极化”），而且用外边的电还能让它的正负极掉个头，这就叫“铁电”。就像您能翻转一块磁铁的南北极一样，但这是电的“极性”。
> 
> 老一辈人用的铁电材料，做薄了就不灵了，就像糖纸太薄容易化。但二维材料皮实，能把“铁电”功能保留在极薄的状态下，这样就能把电子元件做得更小，让手机电脑跑得更快，还省电。
> 
> * * *
> 
> ### 二、咱一段一段地细细讲
> 
> #### 1. 摘要部分（开头那段）
> 
> **洋文原文：**
> 
> > The investigation of two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectrics has attracted significant interest in recent years for applications in functional electronics. Without the limitation of a finite size effect, 2D materials with stable layered structures and reduced surface energy may go beyond the presence of an enhanced depolarization field in ultrathin ferroelectrics, thereby opening a pathway to explore low-dimensional ferroelectricity, making ultra-high-density devices possible and maintaining Moore’s Law. ... Finally, an outlook for future trends and development in 2D ferroelectricity are explored.
> 
> **给您翻译成大白话：** 最近几年，好多科学家都在研究“二维铁电材料”，想用它来做更高级的电子元件。以前的材料一做得太薄，就会因为“尺寸效应”和“退极化场”这两个捣蛋鬼，把“铁电”的本事给弄没了。啥是“退极化场”？您就当成材料内部自己产生的一个相反电场，专门破坏原有的极性，越薄破坏力越强。而二维材料呢，天生是层状结构，表面很稳定，能扛住这个破坏力，让铁电性在极薄状态下还能存在。这样一来，我们就能把电子元件做得更密，继续让芯片越来越强，也就是延续“摩尔定律”——这个定律是说，芯片上的元件数量每两年翻一倍，性能越来越强，但传统材料快做不下去了，二维材料可能是个救星。
> 
> 文章里还总结了：他们先回顾了理论的进展和实验的发现，包括那些“面内”（沿着层方向）和“面外”（垂直于层方向）的极性。然后介绍了各种观测方法，还聊了材料为啥能有铁电性，最后展望了未来咋用。
> 
> #### 2. 引言部分（Introduction）
> 
> **洋文原文开头：**
> 
> > Ferroelectric materials, which are functional materials with stable and switchable spontaneous polarization that can be reversed by an external electric field, are technologically important for many applications...
> 
> **给您讲：** 这段主要讲铁电材料的历史。远在1920年，一个叫Valasek的人，在一种叫“罗谢尔盐”（有点像泻药）的晶体里，第一次发现了铁电现象。后来人们又陆续在别的东西里发现。到了上世纪40年代，有个叫Ginzburg的科学家，用一套理论解释了这种相变（就是材料从一种状态变成另一种状态，比如从普通状态变成铁电状态）。50年代，他们能在金属上长出钙钛矿结构的铁电薄膜了。钙钛矿是一种特殊的晶体结构，很多铁电体都是这种结构，像盖房子的砖块排列方式。
> 
> 但是，问题来了：薄膜变薄的时候，比如薄到几十纳米（1纳米=百万分之一毫米），因为“尺寸效应”，它的铁电性就弱了，甚至没了。这个“尺寸效应”是说，东西太小，表面和界面的影响就大了，内部的合作效应减弱，导致极性不容易保持。就像一群人拉手跳舞，人太少就跳不起来了。
> 
> 后来，科学家们在一种叫PVDF的塑料里，做出了只有几个纳米厚的铁电膜，甚至在1998年，实现了一个分子层（约1纳米）厚的铁电性！这好比用一张纸搭了个能通电的桥，太厉害了。这之后，大家就疯狂研究怎么在传统铁电氧化物（比如PbTiO3、BaTiO3）里也做到这么薄，发现只要基底选得好，工艺用得巧，到几个原子层厚也能有铁电性。
> 
> **再往后，引出二维材料：** 2004年，有个大发现，科学家从石墨里撕出了单层碳原子——石墨烯，就跟撕胶带似的。这打开了二维材料的大门。这些材料层内原子靠强化学键连着，层与层之间靠很弱的“范德华力”粘着，就像一堆扑克牌，牌面很结实，但牌之间很容易滑动。这种结构让它们不容易受尺寸效应影响，所以在极薄时可能还有铁电性。理论物理学家们算出了很多有潜力的二维铁电材料，比如什么1T-MoS2、In2Se3、CuInP2S6等等。有的材料里，铁电性甚至在室温下就能稳定存在，比传统材料强多了。
> 
> **专业词解释：**
> 
> -   **范德华力**：一种很弱的分子间吸引力，就像两张纸轻轻贴在一起，很容易分开。
> -   **居里温度**：铁电材料能保持铁电性的最高温度。超过这个温度，极性就混乱了，铁电性消失。就像冰在零度以上会化成水一样，铁电材料在居里温度以上会变成没有极性的“顺电态”。
> 
> #### 3. 作者简介（文章中插的那段）
> 
> 这里介绍了几个中国科学家，都挺厉害。比如赵冠（Zhao Guan）是博士研究生，研究铁电材料的动力学；钟妮（Ni Zhong）是副教授，研究铁电薄膜和新型器件；段纯刚（Chungang Duan）是教授，当过主任，研究功能材料。他们都在华东师范大学的一个重点实验室工作。
> 
> #### 4. 第二章：二维铁电材料（2. 2D Ferroelectric Materials）
> 
> 这一章很大，分两部分：一是理论上算出来的，二是实验上真做出来的。
> 
> **2.1 为啥理论上算出那么多二维铁电材料？** 因为二维材料维度低，很多原来对称的结构被打破了，自然就容易产生极性。比如，单层MoS2原来中心对称，但稍微变形一下，正负电荷中心不重合了，就有了极性。还有像In2Se3，它的原子排列天生就是不对称的，一层硒夹着两层铟，上下距离不一样，于是就产生了垂直于层的极性，同时面内也有极性，这叫做“偶极锁定”，就像门栓一样，一个方向锁住了，另一个方向也跟着动。还有像CuInP2S6，铜离子在笼子里可以往一边跑，造成极性。
> 
> **2.2 实验上已经证实了哪些二维铁电材料？** 这部分挑了几个典型例子给您讲。
> 
> -   **CuInP2S6（铜铟硫代磷酸盐）**：这个材料里，铜离子像个不安分的小孩，在晶体框架里往一边挤，就形成了极性。2015年，科学家用压电力显微镜（一种能看极性的显微镜）看到了50纳米厚的这个材料里的铁电畴（就是极性一致的小区域），还能翻转它。后来做到4纳米厚，甚至室温下都有铁电性。
> -   **In2Se3（硒化铟）**：这材料有好多相，其中α相在室温下稳定。2017年，科学家在10纳米厚的薄片里看到了铁电畴，而且发现它的面内和面外极性是联动的，翻一个，另一个也跟着转。这就像你转一个魔方，连带旁边的块也动。甚至有实验发现，它薄到1.2纳米（一个原子层）还有铁电性，比其他材料都厉害。
> -   **SnTe（碲化锡）**：2016年，科学家用扫描隧道显微镜（能看到原子的显微镜），在只有一个原子层厚的SnTe里，发现了铁电性，不过是在极低温下。它的原子排列稍微变形，从正方形变成了平行四边形，于是产生了面内极性。这个发现很轰动，因为这么薄还能有铁电性，而且能用探针去翻转它。
> -   **WTe2（碲化钨）**：这是个金属，通常金属里自由电子太多，会把极性屏蔽掉。但2018年，科学家发现WTe2在双层或三层时，竟然是铁电金属！它的极性可能来自电子和空穴的特殊关联，而不是原子位移。这打破了传统认知，很有意思。
> -   **d1T-MoTe2（二碲化钼的一种变形相）**：2019年，科学家在单层MoTe2里发现了室温铁电性。它的原子排列呈三聚体，像三个一组抱团，打破了对称，产生极性。这个发现发表在《自然·通讯》上，很新。
> -   **BA2PbCl4（一种有机无机杂化钙钛矿）**：这个材料是二维钙钛矿，里面的有机阳离子和铅离子偏移，产生了面内极性。它还能在柔性基底上工作，弯曲一下就能发电，很有应用前景。
> 
> #### 5. 第三章：怎么证明二维材料有铁电性？（3. How to Obtain Proofs Indicating 2D Ferroelectricity）
> 
> 这部分讲了几种厉害的“照妖镜”，能把铁电性照出来。
> 
> -   **PFM（压电力显微镜）**：用一根导电的微小探针，一边加电，一边测材料的形变。铁电材料加电压会伸缩（压电效应），探针就能感觉到，还能画出极性分布图，甚至写出“字”来。这是最常用的方法。
> -   **SHG（二次谐波产生）**：一种光学方法。打一束激光到材料上，如果材料“不对称”（即没有中心对称），就会产生倍频光（频率加倍的光）。铁电材料一定是非对称的，所以SHG信号强，就说明有极性，而且能看翻转。这方法不用电极，很干净。
> -   **STM（扫描隧道显微镜）**：能直接看到原子，还能测电子的状态。SnTe的工作就是用它做的，直接看到了原子变形和能带弯曲。
> -   **TEM（透射电子显微镜）**：能看原子排列，确定是哪个相，比如MoTe2的三聚体结构就是用它看到的。
> 
> #### 6. 第四章：二维铁电性的内在机理（4. Intrinsic Mechanisms of 2D Ferroelectricity）
> 
> 为啥会有铁电性？总结起来主要有两类：
> 
> -   **层内键合**：一种是离子位移，比如正负离子相对移动，搞出偶极矩；另一种是共价键重构，比如In2Se3里，硒原子左右移动，旧键断裂、新键生成，导致极性翻转；还有电子极化，比如电子云不对称分布。
> -   **层间相互作用**：比如两层材料之间，一层相对另一层滑移一下，电荷转移就产生了垂直极性。比如双层BN，平移一下就有极性了。
> 
> #### 7. 第五章：应用与挑战（5. Applications and Challenges of 2D Ferroelectric Materials）
> 
> 这些材料能干啥？主要用在存储器、场效应晶体管、隧道结等。比如，做一个铁电隧道结，中间是铁电层，两边是电极，极性翻转时，电阻会变，一个状态是高阻（0），一个状态是低阻（1），这不就是存储器嘛！而且功耗低，速度快。还可以和现在的硅基半导体结合，做成二维器件，比如用MoS2做沟道，用铁电材料做栅介质，开关比很高。还有一些新奇特性，比如铁电性控制自旋（电子自转方向），以后可能做出更神奇的器件。
> 
> 但挑战也不少：实验上真正证实的二维铁电材料还很少，很多机理尚不清楚，测量时容易有假象，比如表面形貌变化、离子移动等，会误导判断。所以还要更小心，结合多种方法验证。
> 
> * * *
> 
> ### 三、按您要求，总结一遍
> 
> **论文题目：** 《二维铁电材料的最新进展》（Recent Progress in Two-Dimensional Ferroelectric Materials）
> 
> **摘要：** 近年来，二维铁电材料因能在极薄尺寸下保持可翻转的自发极化，成为实现超高密度电子器件的候选者，有望延续摩尔定律。文章回顾了理论和实验上的二维铁电材料，包括面内和面外极化，总结了各种表征方法，讨论了内在机制与应用，并展望了未来。
> 
> **研究问题：** 传统铁电材料在纳米尺度下由于尺寸效应和退极化场，铁电性会减弱甚至消失。如何利用二维层状材料突破这一限制，在单原子层厚度下实现稳定的、可翻转的铁电性，并探索其机理和应用。
> 
> **方法：** 这是一篇综述，不是做实验。作者通过广泛查阅文献，总结了：
> 
> -   理论预测：基于密度泛函理论（DFT）等第一性原理计算，预测了多种二维铁电材料。
> -   实验验证：介绍了压电力显微镜（PFM）、二次谐波产生（SHG）、扫描隧道显微镜（STM）、透射电子显微镜（TEM）等先进表征手段，如何证明铁电性。
> -   机理分析：归纳了结构畸变、偶极锁定、电子极化、层间滑移等多种铁电性来源。
> 
> **结论：**
> 
> -   理论上已预测了大量二维铁电材料，但实验上证实的极少，目前只有α-In2Se3、CuInP2S6、SnTe、d1T-MoTe2、WTe2、BA2PbCl4等少数几种。
> -   铁电性可以存在于面内或面外，甚至可以协同锁定。
> -   内在机理多样，包括离子位移、共价键重构、电子极化、层间相对位移等。
> -   这些材料在非易失性存储器、场效应晶体管、隧道结等方面有巨大潜力，但挑战是实验证据获取困难，假象多，需要多手段结合验证。
> 
> **创新点：**
> 
> -   系统梳理了二维铁电材料从理论预测到实验验证的完整脉络。
> -   详细比较了各种表征方法的优缺点，并指出PFM等方法的陷阱。
> -   归纳了二维铁电性的多种产生机制，并特别关注了金属铁电体、偶极锁定等新现象。
> -   提出了未来研究方向，如调控层间作用、开发多铁材料等。
> 
> **不足之处：**
> 
> -   这是一篇综述，本身不包含原创实验，受限于当时已发表的工作。
> -   实验部分总结的材料较少，因为当时证实的确实不多，可能遗漏了一些新兴材料。
> -   对一些机理的讨论仍显浅显，因为很多机理尚在争议中。
> -   应用部分还比较概念化，离实际工业化有距离。
>
